### [1] [Task-Oriented Wireless Transmission of 3D Point Clouds: Geometric Versus Semantic Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13560)
*Vukan Ninkovic,Tamara Sobot,Vladimir Vincan,Gorana Gojic,Dragisa Miskovic,Dejan Vukobratovic*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出面向3D点云传输的端到端语义通信框架，研究几何重建保真度与语义鲁棒性在信道损伤下的关系，发现语义推理在几何质量显著下降时仍保持稳定


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业协作机器人系统需要无线传输高维3D点云，传统压缩方法优先考虑几何保真度，但许多实际应用最终依赖可靠的任务级推理而非精确坐标重建

Method: 提出端到端语义通信框架，联合支持从共享传输表示中进行几何恢复和物体分类，实现坐标级与任务级对噪声敏感性的直接比较

Result: 在真实工业数据集上的实验评估显示显著不对称性：即使几何重建质量显著下降，语义推理在广泛的信噪比范围内仍保持稳定

Conclusion: 可靠的任务执行不需要高保真度的几何恢复，为带宽和功率受限的工业环境中的面向任务的无线感知系统提供了设计见解

Abstract: Wireless transmission of high-dimensional 3D point clouds (PCs) is increasingly required in industrial collaborative robotics systems. Conventional compression methods prioritize geometric fidelity, although many practical applications ultimately depend on reliable task-level inference rather than exact coordinate reconstruction. In this paper, we propose an end-to-end semantic communication framework for wireless 3D PC transmission and conduct a systematic study of the relationship between geometric reconstruction fidelity and semantic robustness under channel impairments. The proposed architecture jointly supports geometric recovery and object classification from a shared transmitted representation, enabling direct comparison between coordinate-level and task-level sensitivity to noise. Experimental evaluation on a real industrial dataset reveals a pronounced asymmetry: semantic inference remains stable across a broad signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) range even when geometric reconstruction quality degrades significantly. These results demonstrate that reliable task execution does not require high-fidelity geometric recovery and provide design insights for task-oriented wireless perception systems in bandwidth- and power-constrained industrial environments.

</details>


### [2] [Airy Beam Engineering in Near-field Communications: A Tractable Closed-Form Analysis in the Terahertz Band](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13866)
*Wenqi Zhao,Chong Han,Emil Björnson*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于太赫兹通信的封闭式艾里波束设计方法，解决准视距场景下的阻塞问题，相比传统波束成形和数值搜索方法具有更好的性能和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太赫兹通信需要大天线阵列，在近场场景中，传统的视距/非视距二元分类不足，准视距场景（视距路径部分阻塞）日益普遍，传统波束成形方法面临挑战。艾里波束具有非衍射和弯曲轨迹特性，但现有研究缺乏实用的封闭式生成方案。

Method: 提出了基于收发器和障碍物位置的封闭式艾里波束相位剖面合成方法。通过严格的电场和轨迹解析推导，建立了ULA艾里波束成形的确定性封闭式设计。利用3D波前可分离性，扩展到UPA阵列，提出混合聚焦-艾里模式和双艾里模式两种操作模式。

Result: 仿真结果验证了推导的轨迹方程有效性，表明所提封闭式设计在准视距场景中显著优于传统波束成形方案。该方法性能与穷举数值搜索相当，但计算复杂度低且物理可解释性更强。

Conclusion: 提出的封闭式艾里波束设计为太赫兹通信中的准视距阻塞问题提供了高效解决方案，平衡了性能、计算复杂度和物理可解释性，为未来无线系统提供了有前景的波束成形方法。

Abstract: Terahertz (THz) communication can offer terabit-per-second rates in future wireless systems, thanks to the ultra-wide bandwidths, but require large antenna arrays. As antenna apertures expand and we enter the near-field scenarios, the conventional binary classification of communication links as either Line-of-Sight (LoS) or Non-Line-of-Sight (NLoS) becomes insufficient. Instead, quasi-LoS scenarios, where the LoS path is partially obstructed, are increasingly prevalent, posing significant challenges for traditional LoS focusing and steering beams. The Airy beam serves as a promising alternative, utilizing its non-diffracting and curved trajectory properties to mitigate such blockages. However, while existing electromagnetics literature primarily explores their physical patterns without practical generation schemes, recent communication-oriented designs predominantly rely on learning-based frameworks lacking interpretable closed-form solutions. To address this issue, this paper investigates a closed-form Airy beam design to efficiently synthesize Airy beam phase profiles based on the positions of the transceivers and obstacles. Specifically, rigorous analytical derivations of the electric field and trajectory are presented to establish a deterministic closed-form design for ULA Airy beamforming. Leveraging 3D wavefront separability, this framework is extended to uniform planar arrays (UPAs) with two operation modes: the hybrid focusing-Airy mode and the dual Airy mode. Simulation results verify the effectiveness of our derived trajectory equations and demonstrate that the proposed closed-form design significantly outperforms conventional beamforming schemes in quasi-LoS scenarios. Furthermore, the proposed method achieves performance comparable to exhaustive numerical searches with low computational complexity and enhanced physical interpretability.

</details>


### [3] [NLOS-Aided Joint OTA Synchronization and Off-Grid Imaging for Distributed MIMO Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13981)
*Xin Tong,Lechen Zhang,Yu Ge,Dario Tagliaferri,Henk Wymeersch*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于分布式MIMO ISAC系统的NLOS辅助联合OTA同步和离网成像框架，通过闭环交替优化实现同步与成像的相互增强


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式MIMO架构虽能实现大规模ISAC，但实际相位相干感知面临相位同步误差和网格离散化导致的建模失配问题。现有OTA同步方法通常将同步和感知任务分开处理，当利用多径分量进行成像时可能导致相位对齐不准确

Method: 1. LOS辅助的粗同步建立分布式链路的初始相位相干性；2. 利用成像结果重建的NLOS分量进行迭代细化；3. 通过一阶泰勒展开建模离网效应，将非线性离网偏移转化为具有联合稀疏反射率和偏移变量的增强线性模型；4. 将成像问题重构为结构化稀疏恢复任务，使用定制的离网近似消息传递算法求解；5. 成像和同步模块在闭环交替优化框架中耦合，相互增强

Result: 数值结果表明，所提框架在相位误差下实现了准确的同步和成像。与传统方法相比，表现出优越的鲁棒性和准确性

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种有效的NLOS辅助联合OTA同步和离网成像框架，通过同步与成像的闭环交替优化解决了分布式MIMO ISAC系统中的相位同步和离网成像挑战，显著提升了系统性能

Abstract: Distributed multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) architectures enable large-scale integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) by providing high spatial resolution and robustness through spatial diversity. However, practical phase-coherent sensing is challenged by phase synchronization errors and modeling mismatch caused by grid discretization. Existing over-the-air (OTA) synchronization methods typically treat synchronization and sensing tasks separately, which may lead to inaccurate phase alignment when multipath components are used for imaging. In this paper, we propose a non-line-of-sight (NLOS)-aided joint OTA synchronization and off-grid imaging framework for distributed MIMO ISAC systems. First, a line-of-sight (LOS)-assisted coarse synchronization is performed to establish initial phase coherence across distributed links. Subsequently, an iterative refinement stage exploits reconstructed NLOS components obtained from imaging results. By modeling off-grid effects via a first-order Taylor expansion, we transform measurements with nonlinear off-grid offset into an augmented linear model with jointly sparse reflectivity and off-set variables. The imaging problem is reformulated as a structured sparse recovery task and solved using a tailored off-grid approximate message passing (OG-AMP) algorithm. The imaging and synchronization modules are coupled within a closed-loop alternative optimization framework, where improved imaging enables more accurate phase refinement, and vice versa. Numerical results show that the proposed framework achieves accurate synchronization and imaging under phase errors. Compared with conventional approaches, it shows superior robustness and accuracy.

</details>


### [4] [The Taxonomies, Training, and Applications of Event Stream Modelling for Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14003)
*Mingcheng Zhu,Yu Liu,Zhiyao Luo,Tingting Zhu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文综述了电子健康记录事件流建模方法，提出了统一的事件流定义和分类体系，系统回顾了训练策略和应用场景，并指出了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录包含异构临床数据，传统多变量时间序列方法难以处理真实临床工作流程的稀疏性和不规则性。现有事件流建模研究存在定义不一致、架构分散、训练协议不统一等问题，需要建立统一框架来指导下一代医疗模型开发。

Method: 建立统一的EHR事件流定义，提出基于事件时间、类型和值处理方式的新型分类体系。系统回顾从监督学习到自监督学习的训练策略，全面讨论临床场景应用。

Result: 提供了事件流建模的清晰分类框架，总结了现有训练方法和技术，分析了不同临床应用场景的适用性，为研究者提供了系统化的参考指南。

Conclusion: 事件流表示能更好地保留患者旅程的精确时间结构，但需要统一的标准和分类体系。未来需要解决开放关键挑战，推动下一代医疗模型发展，该综述为当前研究格局提供了澄清和指导。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of electronic health records (EHRs) enables the acquisition of heterogeneous clinical data, spanning lab tests, vital signs, medications, and procedures, which offer transformative potential for artificial intelligence in healthcare. Although traditional modelling approaches have typically relied on multivariate time series, they often struggle to accommodate the inherent sparsity and irregularity of real-world clinical workflows. Consequently, research has shifted toward event stream representation, which treats patient records as continuous sequences, thereby preserving the precise temporal structure of the patient journey. However, the existing literature remains fragmented, characterised by inconsistent definitions, disparate modelling architectures, and varying training protocols. To address these gaps, this review establishes a unified definition of EHR event streams and introduces a novel taxonomy that categorises models based on their handling of event time, type, and value. We systematically review training strategies, ranging from supervised learning to self-supervised methods, and provide a comprehensive discussion of applications across clinical scenarios. Finally, we identify open critical challenges and future directions, with the aim of clarifying the current landscape and guiding the development of next-generation healthcare models.

</details>


### [5] [Geometric Framework for Robust Order Detection in Delay-Coordinates Dynamic Mode Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14383)
*Yoav Harris,Hadas Benisty,Ronen Talmon*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出基于子空间几何的DC-DMD模型阶数检测和模式选择框架，通过几何残差和KV结构一致性区分真实模式与虚假模式


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DC-DMD在提取高维时间序列的相干时空模式时面临关键挑战：如何区分由噪声和阶数过估计引起的虚假模式与具有动态意义的真实模式

Method: 1) 基于子空间几何的框架：真实模式集中在低维信号子空间内，虚假模式在信号子空间外有不可忽略分量；2) 几何残差评分；3) 基于块伴随矩阵的算子理论分析，揭示所有模式都具有Kronecker-Vandermonde结构，真实模式更符合该结构

Result: 提出的几何和结构方法在噪声水平、谱分离、阻尼机制和嵌入长度等条件下，相比现有基线方法实现了更鲁棒和准确的阶数检测与模式选择

Conclusion: DC-DMD中的模型阶数检测和模式选择本质上是子空间几何问题，几何框架提供了扰动鲁棒的定义和完全数据驱动的选择准则，同时为幅度和范数启发式方法的经验行为提供了理论解释

Abstract: Delay-coordinates dynamic mode decomposition (DC-DMD) is widely used to extract coherent spatiotemporal modes from high-dimensional time series. A central challenge is distinguishing dynamically meaningful modes from spurious modes induced by noise and order overestimation. We show that model order detection and mode selection in DC-DMD are fundamentally problems of subspace geometry. Specifically, true modes are characterized by concentration within a low-dimensional signal subspace, whereas spurious modes necessarily retain non-negligible components outside any moderate overestimate of that subspace. This geometric distinction yields a perturbation-robust definition of true and spurious modes and yields fully data-driven selection criteria. This geometric framework leads to two complementary data-driven selection criteria. The first is derived directly from the geometric distinction and uses a data-driven proxy of the signal-subspace to compute a residual score. The second arises from a new operator-theoretic analysis of delay embedding. Using a block-companion formulation, we show that all modes exhibit a Kronecker-Vandermonde (KV) structure induced by the delay-coordinates, and true modes are distinguished by the degree to which they conform to it. Importantly, we also show that this deviation is governed precisely by the geometric residual. In addition, our analysis provides a principled explanation for the empirical behavior of magnitude- and norm-based heuristics, clarifying when and why they fail under delay-coordinates. Extensive numerical experiments confirm the theoretical predictions and demonstrate that the proposed geometric and structure-based methods achieve robust and accurate order detection and mode selection, consistently better than existing baselines across noise levels, spectral separations, damping regimes, and embedding lengths.

</details>


### [6] [Near-Field Channel Estimation for mmWave/THz Communications with Extremely Large-Scale UPAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14437)
*Yiming Chen,Hongwei Wang,Lingxiang Li,Zhi Chen*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对均匀平面阵列近场信道估计的二维块稀疏结构建模方法，通过构建改进的二维DFT字典并采用2D-PCSBL算法，相比现有方法获得了更好的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波/太赫兹通信中广泛采用极大规模天线阵列来补偿严重的路径损耗，但信道估计面临挑战，因为ELAAs的瑞利距离延伸到数十甚至数百米，需要考虑近场信道模型。现有的基于极坐标域的方法和基于块稀疏的方法最初是为均匀线性阵列设计的，前者应用于UPAs时性能平庸，后者无法直接扩展到UPA场景。

Method: 首先将原始UPA近场信道重新表述为两个ULA近场信道的外积，并为其构建改进的二维DFT字典。证明UPA近场信道具有二维块稀疏结构，利用这种特定稀疏结构，采用二维模式耦合稀疏贝叶斯学习算法解决信道估计问题。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的方法在保持可比计算复杂度的同时，优于传统的现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过揭示UPA近场信道的二维块稀疏特性并设计相应的估计算法，有效解决了UPA场景下的近场信道估计问题，为极大规模天线阵列系统提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Extremely large antenna arrays (ELAAs) are widely adopted in mmWave/THz communications to compensate for the severe path loss, wherein the channel estimation remains a significant challenge since the Rayleigh distance of ELAAs stretches to tens or even hundreds of meters and the near-field channel model should be considered. Existing polar-domain based methods and block-sparse based methods are originally devised for Uniform Linear Arrays (ULAs) near-field channel estimation. The polar-domain based method can be applied to Uniform Planar Arrays (UPAs), but it behaves plain since it ignores the specific sparsity structure of the UPA near-field channels. Meanwhile, the block-sparse based method cannot be extended to the UPA scenarios directly. To address these issues, we first reformulate the original UPA near-field channel as an outer product of two ULA near-field channels and we construct a modified two-dimensional DFT (2D-DFT) dictionary for it. With the proposed dictionary, we further prove that the UPA near-field channel admits a 2D block-sparse structure. Leveraging this specific sparse structure, we solve the channel estimation problem with the 2D Pattern-Coupled Sparse Bayesian Learning (2D-PCSBL) algorithm. Simulation results show that the proposed approach outperforms conventional existing methods while maintaining a comparable computational complexity.

</details>


### [7] [A Decoupling-based Approach for Signature Estimation of Wideband XL MIMO-FMCW Radars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14542)
*Chandrashekhar Rai,Dibbendu Roy,Debarati Sen*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于解耦的目标检测与参数估计框架，用于解决XL-MIMO FMCW雷达中空间宽带效应引起的距离-角度耦合问题，通过顺序频率估计实现高分辨率目标分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代雷达采用宽带信号和XL-MIMO阵列可显著提高距离和角度分辨率，但同时使用大带宽和大阵列孔径时，空间延迟与雷达距离分辨率相当，导致空间宽带效应。SWE引起距离偏移、波束偏移和距离-角度耦合等失真，使目标响应在距离-角度域扩散，可能导致物理分离的目标重叠和相互掩蔽。

Method: 提出解耦基的目标检测与参数估计框架，将联合距离-角度估计问题重新表述为解耦的顺序频率估计问题，通过连续的一维超分辨率估计完成二维估计。具体采用正交匹配追踪进行基于稀疏恢复的距离和角度估计，并将该解耦策略扩展到空间宽带XL-MIMO FMCW雷达系统。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提方法能准确检测多个目标，并在存在SWE的情况下成功解析重叠的目标响应，性能优于传统的傅里叶变换和基于聚类的方法。

Conclusion: 提出的解耦框架有效解决了XL-MIMO FMCW雷达中的距离-角度耦合问题，即使在严重RAC导致目标响应重叠的情况下，也能实现可靠的目标检测和分离，为空间宽带雷达系统提供了有效的信号处理解决方案。

Abstract: Modern radars employing wideband signals and extremely large (XL) multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) arrays can significantly improve range and angular resolution. However, when large bandwidth and array aperture are used simultaneously, the spatial delay across the array becomes comparable to the radar range resolution, leading to the spatial wideband effect (SWE). The SWE introduces several distortions including range migration (range squint), beam squint, and range-angle coupling (RAC), which spread the target response in the range-angle domain and may cause physically separated targets to overlap and mask each other. In this work, we propose a decoupling-based target detection and parameter estimation framework for MIMO frequency modulated continuous wave (FMCW) radar. The proposed method reformulates the joint range-angle estimation problem as a decoupled sequential frequency estimation problem, where the two-dimensional (2D) estimation is carried out through successive one-dimensional (1D) super-resolution estimations. Specifically, we employ orthogonal matching pursuit (OMP) to perform sparse recovery-based range and angle estimation with high resolution. The proposed decoupling strategy is further extended to spatial wideband XL-MIMO FMCW radar systems, enabling reliable detection and separation of targets even when their responses overlap due to severe RAC. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed approach accurately detects multiple targets and successfully resolves overlapping target responses in the presence of SWE, outperforming conventional Fourier transform and clustering-based methods.

</details>


### [8] [A Spatio-Temporal-Frequency Transformer Framework for Near-Field Target Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14829)
*Zongyao Zhao,Zhaolin Wang,Lincong Han,Jing Jin,Kaibin Huang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出基于近场ISAC系统的目标识别框架，利用近场球面波前提供的距离相关空间特征，在带宽受限条件下实现高精度感知


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统远场ISAC系统在带宽受限条件下感知精度受限，而近场球面波前包含距离相关的空间特征，可提供更丰富的目标信息，但需要有效的特征提取和建模方法

Method: 提出时空频变换器框架：1) 轻量级空间编码器从天线阵列提取每帧和子载波特征；2) 时间-频率变换器头融合特征，使用位置嵌入建模时间动态和跨子载波相关性

Result: 仿真结果表明，即使在有限带宽资源下，该框架也能实现强大的目标识别性能

Conclusion: 近场ISAC系统结合时空频变换器框架，通过利用近场球面波前的距离相关空间特征，可在带宽受限条件下实现高效准确的目标识别

Abstract: A target recognition framework relying on near-field integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) systems is proposed. By exploiting the distance-dependent spatial signatures provided by the near-field spherical wavefront, high-accuracy sensing is realized in a bandwidth-efficient manner. A spatio--temporal--frequency (STF) transformer framework is introduced for target recognition using electromagnetic features found in the wireless channel response. In particular, a lightweight spatial encoder is employed to extract features from the antenna array for each frame and subcarrier. These features are then fused by a time-frequency transformer head with positional embeddings to model temporal dynamics and cross-subcarrier correlations. Simulation results demonstrate that strong target recognition performance can be achieved even with limited bandwidth resources.

</details>


### [9] [Flag-Preamble-Based Delay-Doppler Channel Estimation for Next-Evolution Waveforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14875)
*Yuan Liu,Yilin Qi,Caiqin Li,Haoran Yin,Yanqun Tang,Yao Ge*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种结合Flag序列优化和信道估计算法的鲁棒信道估计方案，通过候选选择策略和全局最小二乘细化提升性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确的时延-多普勒信道估计对下一代演进波形至关重要，需要解决传统贪婪估计的鲁棒性问题

Method: 1) 使用自适应加速并行主-最小化算法优化Flag序列；2) 提出候选选择策略抑制虚假旁瓣峰值；3) 引入全局最小二乘细化阶段消除旁瓣掩蔽效应导致的误差传播

Result: 数值结果表明，所提方案显著优于传统现有算法，达到了期望的估计精度

Conclusion: 提出的鲁棒信道估计算法通过集成优化的Flag序列、候选选择策略和全局LS细化，有效解决了传统方法的鲁棒性问题，为下一代演进波形提供了可靠的信号检测支持

Abstract: Accurate delay-Doppler channel estimation is critical for next-evolution waveforms (NEWs) to enable reliable signal detection. This paper proposes a robust channel estimation algorithm that integrates Flag sequences optimized via an adaptive accelerated parallel majorization-minimization (AP-MM) algorithm with a proposed channel estimation algorithm. To enable efficient, low-complexity parameter extraction and further overcome the robustness issues of conventional greedy estimation, we introduce two key enhancements, i.e., a candidate selection strategy to mitigate spurious sidelobe peaks, and a global least squares (LS) refinement stage to eliminate error propagation caused by sidelobe masking effects. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed scheme significantly outperforms traditional existing algorithms, achieving the desired estimation accuracy.

</details>


### [10] [Integrated Channel Sounding and Communication: Requirements, Architecture, Challenges, and Key Technologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14912)
*Nanhao Zhou,Chao Zou,Yu Zhou,Yanqun Tang,Xiaoying Zhang,Haoran Yin,Xuefeng Yin,Yuxiang Zhang,Dan Fei,Fan Jiang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出ICSC框架，通过深度集成信道探测与通信，实现动态信道特性的实时高效获取，支持细粒度场景特定测量，利用AI技术实时识别信道条件并自适应调整波形参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统信道探测方法存在局限性：测量活动泛化、跨频段一致性不足、实时适应性不够，无法满足SAGSIN对场景特定和高精度信道建模的需求。

Method: 提出集成信道探测与通信（ICSC）技术框架，将探测与通信深度集成，在通信过程中实时获取动态信道特性，利用人工智能技术识别信道条件并自适应调整波形参数。

Result: 开发了集成验证系统（IVS）验证了ICSC框架的可行性，分析了ICSC的潜在应用和机遇，讨论了未来发展方向和剩余挑战。

Conclusion: ICSC框架能够解决传统信道探测方法的局限性，为SAGSIN提供场景特定、高精度的信道建模，通过AI驱动的实时自适应增强通信性能。

Abstract: Channel models are essential for the design, evaluation, and optimization of wireless communication systems. The emerging space-air-ground-sea integrated network (SAGSIN), characterized by diverse service applications and extended-spectrum operations, places even greater demands on highly accurate channel models. However, conventional channel sounding is limited by generalized measurement campaigns, inadequate cross-band consistency, and insufficient real-time adaptability, making it unable to meet the needs of SAGSIN for scenario-specific and high-precision channel modeling. To address this challenge, we propose a novel technological framework, termed integrated channel sounding and communication (ICSC). By deeply integrating sounding and communication, the ICSC enables efficient and real-time acquisition of dynamic channel characteristics during communication processes, supporting fine-grained site- and scenario-specific measurements. Furthermore, leveraging artificial intelligence techniques, ICSC can identify channel conditions and adapt waveform parameters in real-time according to scenario variations, which in turn enhances communication performance. This article first introduces the fundamental principles of the ICSC framework, elaborates on its core concepts and key advantages, and demonstrates its feasibility through the development of an integrated verification system (IVS). Subsequently, the potential applications and opportunities of the ICSC are analyzed in depth, followed by a discussion of its future development directions and remaining challenges.

</details>


### [11] [Cyclic Delay-Doppler Shift: A Simple Transmit Diversity Technique for Ultra-Reliable Communications in Doubly Selective Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14959)
*Haoran Yin,Yu Zhou,Yanqun Tang,Di Zhang,Chi Zhang,Xizhang Wei,Jiaojiao Xiong,Fan Liu,Marwa Chafii,Mérouane Debbah*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为循环延迟-多普勒偏移(CDDS)的简单发射分集技术，用于AFDM和OTFS波形，以在双选择性信道中实现超可靠通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AFDM和OTFS是两种有前景的先进波形，适用于高移动性场景下的可靠通信。然而，在双选择性信道中实现超可靠通信需要有效的分集技术。传统发射分集技术存在信道估计开销大、实现复杂和处理延迟高等问题，因此需要一种更简单高效的方案。

Method: 提出了两种简单的CDDS方案：调制域CDDS(MD-CDDS)和时间域CDDS(TD-CDDS)，分别在调制前和调制后在发射端执行CDDS。两种方案都可以通过将发射向量与精心设计的预编码矩阵相乘来实现，该预编码矩阵本质上是稀疏的相位补偿置换矩阵。

Result: 理论分析和数值仿真证明，当采用适当的CDDS步长时，CDDS可以为MIMO-AFDM和MIMO-OTFS提供最优的发射分集增益。与传统发射分集技术相比，CDDS方案具有更低的信道估计开销、实现复杂度和信号处理延迟。

Conclusion: CDDS是一种简单高效的发射分集技术，特别适用于高移动性场景下的超可靠通信。该技术通过简单的预编码矩阵实现，为AFDM和OTFS波形提供了最优的发射分集增益，同时保持了低复杂度和低延迟的优势。

Abstract: Affine frequency division multiplexing (AFDM) and orthogonal time frequency space (OTFS) are two promising advanced waveforms proposed for reliable communications in high-mobility scenarios. In this paper, we introduce a simple transmit diversity technique, termed cyclic delay-Doppler shift (CDDS), for these two advanced waveforms to achieve ultra-reliable communications in doubly selective channels (DSCs). Two simple CDDS schemes, named modulation-domain CDDS (MD-CDDS) and time-domain CDDS (TD-CDDS), are proposed, which perform CDDS in advance at the transmitter before and after the modulation, respectively. We demonstrate that both of the two proposed CDDS schemes can be implemented efficiently and flexibly by multiplying the transmit vector with a well-designed precoding matrix, which is nothing but a sparse phase-compensated permutation matrix. Moreover, we theoretically and numerically prove that CDDS can provide MIMO-AFDM and MIMO-OTFS with optimal transmit diversity gain when a proper CDDS step is adopted. Compared to the conventional transmit diversity techniques, the proposed CDDS scheme enjoys the advantages of lower channel estimation overhead, implementation complexity, and signal processing latency, making it particularly suitable for ultra-reliable communications in high-mobility scenarios.

</details>


### [12] [Generative Semantic HARQ: Latent-Space Text Retransmission and Combining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15068)
*Bin Han,Yulin Hu,Hans D. Schotten*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于文本通信的语义级混合自动重传请求框架，使用Transformer-VAE编解码器作为传统协议栈的轻量级覆盖层，通过随机编码器生成多样化的潜在表示来实现增量知识传输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义通信关注的是意义而非原始比特的传输，但语义级的可靠性仍然是一个未解决的挑战。传统通信协议主要保证比特级可靠性，而语义通信需要新的机制来确保语义内容的可靠传输。

Method: 1. 使用Transformer-VAE编解码器作为传统协议栈的轻量级覆盖层；2. 随机编码器在重传时生成多样化的潜在表示，实现增量知识传输；3. 接收端使用软质量估计器触发重传；4. 质量感知组合器在一致的潜在空间内合并接收到的潜在向量；5. 系统评估了六种语义质量指标和四种软组合策略。

Result: 在混合语义失真（系统偏差与加性噪声混合）条件下，加权平均或MRC启发式组合与基于自一致性的HARQ触发机制相结合表现最佳。该方法能够在单一模型中实现增量知识传输，无需专门的协议设计。

Conclusion: 提出的语义级HARQ框架通过Transformer-VAE编解码器实现了语义通信的可靠传输，随机编码器的多样化潜在表示提供了增量知识，软组合策略与质量触发机制的结合显著提升了语义通信的可靠性。

Abstract: Semantic communication conveys meaning rather than raw bits, but reliability at the semantic level remains an open challenge. We propose a semantic-level hybrid automatic repeat request (HARQ) framework for text communication, in which a Transformer-variational autoencoder (VAE) codec operates as a lightweight overlay on the conventional protocol stack. The stochastic encoder inherently generates diverse latent representations across retransmissions-providing incremental knowledge (IK) from a single model without dedicated protocol design. On the receiver side, a soft quality estimator triggers retransmissions and a quality-aware combiner merges the received latent vectors within a consistent latent space. We systematically benchmark six semantic quality metrics and four soft combining strategies under hybrid semantic distortion that mixes systematic bias with additive noise. The results suggest combining Weighted-Average or MRC-Inspired combining with self-consistency-based HARQ triggering for the best performance.

</details>


### [13] [Beam Prediction Based on Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15093)
*Tianhao Mao,Le Liang,Jie Yang,Xiao Li,Shi Jin*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出基于多模态大语言模型的波束预测框架，利用RGB相机图像和LiDAR点云等传感器数据，通过专门设计的模态编码器、波束引导注意力掩码机制和高频时序对齐策略实现跨模态特征融合，在构建的大规模多模态数据集上显著提升波束预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的波束预测是下一代无线通信系统的关键使能技术。传统方法依赖理想的离开角知识，在实际动态环境中性能受限。多模态传感器数据（如相机图像和LiDAR点云）提供了丰富的上下文信息，但如何有效融合这些异构模态并应用于波束预测仍具挑战。

Method: 1. 设计基于多模态大语言模型的波束预测框架；2. 开发专门的模态编码器处理RGB图像和LiDAR点云；3. 提出波束引导注意力掩码机制；4. 引入高频时序对齐策略确保跨模态特征在动态环境下的鲁棒集成；5. 构建大规模多模态无线通信数据集Multimodal-Wireless，涵盖多种天气和交通条件，包含高精度射线追踪标签。

Result: 1. 显著减少对理想离开角知识的依赖；2. 在波束准确性和通信性能方面持续优于现有最先进的多模态LLM基波束预测方法；3. 平均Top-1准确率提升至80.8%；4. 平均归一化增益达到89.1%。

Conclusion: 提出的多模态LLM基波束预测框架通过有效融合异构传感器数据，在动态环境中实现了鲁棒的跨模态特征集成，显著提升了波束预测性能，为下一代无线通信系统提供了有力的技术支撑。

Abstract: Accurate beam prediction is a key enabler for next-generation wireless communication systems. In this paper, we propose a multimodal large language model (LLM)-based beam prediction framework that effectively utilizes contextual information, provided by sensory data including RGB camera images and LiDAR point clouds. To effectively fuse heterogeneous modalities, we design specialized modality encoders together with a beam-guided attention masking mechanism and a high-frequency temporal alignment strategy, enabling robust cross-modal feature integration under dynamic environments. Furthermore, we construct a large-scale multimodal dataset for communication, named Multimodal-Wireless, which covers diverse weather and traffic conditions with high-fidelity ray-tracing labels. Extensive simulation results demonstrate that the proposed approach significantly reduces the reliance on oracle angle-of-departure knowledge and consistently outperforms state-of-the-art multimodal LLM-based beam prediction methods in terms of beam accuracy and communication performance, improving the average Top-1 accuracy to 80.8% and the average normalized gain to 89.1%.

</details>


### [14] [Dual-Domain Sparse Adaptive Filtering: Exploiting Error Memory for Improved Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15105)
*Mohammad Salman,Hadi Zayyani,Felipe A. P. de Figueiredo,Hasan Abu Hilal,Mostafa Rashdan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出双域稀疏自适应滤波器(DD-SAF)，通过同时考虑系数大小和误差记忆向量来早期识别活跃系数，解决传统零吸引算法在初始收敛阶段错误惩罚小但活跃系数的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统零吸引自适应滤波器仅根据系数当前大小决定惩罚对象，导致初始适应阶段活跃系数（虽小但应增长）与真正不活跃系数难以区分，算法对需要发展的系数施加强惩罚，减缓了初始收敛速度。

Method: 提出双域方法：同时从两个视角观察系数。除了跟踪系数大小外，引入误差记忆向量，监控每个系数随时间对适应误差的持续贡献程度。持续出现在误差信号中的系数即使很小也可能是活跃的。结合两种视角，DD-SAF能够早期识别活跃系数并相应消除惩罚。

Result: 理论分析表明DD-SAF保持与标准LMS相同的稳定性，同时证明比现有方法具有更好的稳态性能。仿真显示在稀疏系统识别场景中，DD-SAF比标准LMS和重加权零吸引LMS(RZA-LMS)更快收敛到稳态和/或达到更低的均方偏差(MSD)。

Conclusion: DD-SAF通过双域方法有效解决了传统零吸引算法在初始收敛阶段的识别问题，实现了更快的收敛速度和更好的稳态性能，为稀疏系统识别提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Many signal processing applications such as acoustic echo cancellation and wireless channel estimation require identifying systems where only a small fraction of coefficients are actually active, i.e. sparse systems. Zero-attracting adaptive filters tackle this by adding a penalty that pulls inactive coefficients toward zero, speeding up convergence. However, these algorithms determine which coefficients to penalize based solely on their current size. This creates a problem during early adaptation since active coefficients that should eventually grow large start out small, making them look identical to truly inactive coefficients. The algorithm ends up applying strong penalties to the very coefficients it needs to develop, slowing down the initial convergence. This paper provides a solution to this problem by introducing a dual-domain approach that looks at coefficients from two perspectives simultaneously. Beyond just tracking coefficient magnitude, we introduce an error-memory vector that monitors how persistently each coefficient contributes to the adaptation error over time. If a coefficient keeps showing up in the error signal, it is probably active even if it is still small. By combining both views, the proposed dual-domain sparse adaptive filter (DD-SAF) can identify active coefficients early and eliminate penalties accordingly. Moreover, complete theoretical analysis is derived. The analysis shows that DD-SAF maintains the same stability properties as standard least-mean-square (LMS) while achieves provably better steady-state performance than existing methods. Simulations demonstrate that the DD-SAF converges to the steady-state faster and/or convergences to a lower mean-square-deviation (MSD) than the standard LMS and the reweighted zero-attracting LMS (RZA-LMS) algorithms for sparse system identification settings.

</details>


### [15] [Fast Volume Alignment by Frequency-Marched Newton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15285)
*Fabian Kruse,Valentin Debarnot,Vinith Kishore,Ivan Dokmanić*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种快速准确的3D对齐方法，通过连续优化而非离散搜索实现高效姿态估计，结合Wigner-D展开、二阶优化和频率渐进策略，显著减少计算时间


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统匹配滤波方法在离散变换空间评估互相关计算量大，需要开发更高效的连续优化方法实现高精度3D对齐

Method: 使用带限Wigner-D展开旋转相关性，实现快速评估和闭式梯度/Hessian计算；采用二阶优化结合频率渐进策略：低分辨率SO(3)粗搜索提供候选，通过迭代频率渐进和牛顿步进行精炼，平移更新采用交替FFT方案

Result: 在合成旋转估计基准测试中达到亚度精度，相比穷举SO(3)搜索显著减少运行时间；集成到RELION5亚断层图平均流程中，保持基线重建质量（达到奈奎斯特极限的局部分辨率），同时将姿态精炼时间减少一个数量级以上

Conclusion: 该方法通过连续优化框架实现了高效准确的3D对齐，结合谱分析和确定性收敛保证，为大规模3D重建任务提供了实用的快速姿态估计解决方案

Abstract: We develop a fast and accurate method for 3D alignment, recovering the rotation and translation that best align a reference volume with a noisy observation. Classical matched filtering evaluates cross-correlation over a large discretized transformation space; we show that high-precision alignment can be achieved far more efficiently by treating pose estimation as a continuous optimization problem. Our starting point is a band-limited Wigner-$D$ expansion of the rotational correlation, which enables rapid evaluation and efficient closed-form gradients and Hessians. Combined with analytical control of the complexity of trigonometric-polynomial landscapes, this makes second-order optimization practical in a setting where it is often avoided due to nonconvexity and noise sensitivity. We show that Newton-type refinement is stable and effective when initialized at low angular bandwidth: a coarse low-resolution $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ search provides robust candidates, which are then refined by iterative frequency marching and Newton steps, with translations updated via FFT in an alternating scheme. We provide a deterministic convergence guarantee showing that, under verifiable spectral-decay and gap conditions, the frequency-marching scheme returns a near-optimal solution whose suboptimality is controlled by the Newton tolerance. On synthetic rotation-estimation benchmarks, the method attains sub-degree accuracy while substantially reducing runtime relative to exhaustive $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ search. Integrated into the subtomogram-averaging pipeline of RELION5, it matches the baseline reconstruction quality, reaching local resolution at the Nyquist limit, while reducing pose-refinement time by more than an order of magnitude.

</details>


### [16] [On the CRLB for Blind Receiver I/Q Imbalance Estimation in OFDM Systems: Efficient Computation and Closed-Form Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15310)
*Moritz Tockner,Oliver Lang,Andreas Meingassner-Lang,Mario Huemer*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该论文扩展了先前会议工作，为盲估计频率无关接收机I/Q不平衡推导了Cramér-Rao下界，并提出了计算高效的边界计算方法，将复杂度从样本数的立方降低到FFT大小的线性复杂度，同时提供了简化的闭式近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接变频接收机架构对I/Q路径的幅度和相位失配敏感，导致基带信号中出现不需要的镜像分量，降低链路性能。现有盲估计I/Q不平衡方法缺乏理论性能界限，而数据辅助估计已有CRLB，因此需要为盲估计情况建立理论性能界限。

Method: 扩展先前会议工作，为频率无关接收机I/Q不平衡的盲估计推导Cramér-Rao下界。提出计算高效的边界计算方法，将复杂度从O(N³)降低到O(N)，其中N为FFT大小。同时提供简化的闭式近似，并基于此提出预估计滤波改进方案。

Result: 成功推导了盲估计I/Q不平衡的CRLB，实现了计算复杂度的显著降低（从立方到线性）。闭式近似提供了对现有估计方法分配依赖性能的新见解，预估计滤波改进在某些场景下大幅提升了估计性能。

Conclusion: 该工作为盲I/Q不平衡估计建立了理论性能界限，提供了高效的计算方法和简化的闭式近似。这些结果为现有估计方法的性能分析提供了理论基础，并通过预估计滤波改进显著提升了实际估计性能。

Abstract: Modern mobile communication receivers are often implemented with a direct-conversion architecture, which features a number of advantages over competing designs. A notable limitation of direct-conversion architectures, however, is their sensitivity to amplitude and phase mismatches between the in-phase and quadrature signal paths. Such in-phase and quadrature-phase (I/Q) imbalances introduce undesired image components in the baseband signal, degrading link performance -- most notably by increasing the bit-error ratio. Considerable research effort has therefore been devoted to digital techniques for estimating and mitigating these impairments. Existing approaches generally fall into two categories: data-aided methods that exploit known pilots, preambles, or training sequences, and blind techniques that operate without such prior information. For data-aided estimation, Cramér-Rao lower bounds (CRLBs) have been established in the literature. In contrast, the derivation of a CRLB for the blind I/Q-imbalance estimation case is considerably more challenging, since the received data is random and typically non-Gaussian in the frequency domain. This work extends our earlier conference contribution, which introduced a CRLB derivation for the blind estimation of frequency-independent (FID) receiver I/Q imbalance using central limit theorem (CLT) arguments. The extensions include a computationally efficient method for calculating the bound, reducing complexity from cubic in the number of samples to linear in the fast-Fourier transform (FFT) size, along with a simplified closed-form approximation. This approximation provides new insights into the allocation dependent performances of existing estimation methods, motivating a pre-estimation filtering modification that drastically improves their estimation performance in certain scenarios.

</details>


### [17] [Near-field Boundary Distance in mmWave and THz Communications with Misaligned Antenna Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15311)
*Peng Zhang,Vitaly Petrov,Emil Björnson*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个用于表征毫米波和太赫兹定向通信系统中辐射近场距离的广义数学框架，考虑了实际阵列旋转和失准情况，推导了紧凑的闭式表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波和太赫兹通信虽然能提供超高数据速率，但其短波长特性导致扩展的辐射近场区域，传统远场假设在此失效。现有研究大多未考虑通信双方都使用天线阵列的情况，或者假设阵列完美对准，这在许多实际部署中不成立，特别是在节点移动时。

Method: 提出了一个广义数学框架来表征定向毫米波和太赫兹通信系统中的辐射近场距离，考虑了各种实际阵列旋转和失准情况。使用该框架推导了紧凑的闭式表达式，适用于多种天线配置，包括阵列到阵列和阵列到点设置，同时考虑了线性和平面阵列。

Result: 数值研究表明，天线失准的存在可能显著调整毫米波和太赫兹通信系统中近场区域的边界。推导的闭式表达式为各种天线配置提供了近场边界距离的计算方法。

Conclusion: 该研究为毫米波和太赫兹通信系统中的近场区域分析提供了更现实的框架，考虑了实际部署中常见的阵列失准问题，这对于系统设计和性能评估具有重要意义。

Abstract: Wireless communications in the millimeter wave (mmWave) and terahertz (THz) spectrum allow harnessing large frequency bands, thus achieving ultra-high data rates. However, the inherently short wavelengths of mmWave and THz signals lead to an extended radiative near-field region, where certain canonical far-field assumptions fail. Most prior works aimed to characterize this radiative near-field region either do not consider antenna arrays on both communicating nodes or, if they do, assume perfect alignment between the arrays. However, such assumptions break down in many realistic deployments, where both sides must employ large-scale mmWave/THz antenna arrays to maintain the desired communication range, while perfect antenna alignment cannot be guaranteed particularly under nodes mobility. In this work, a generalized mathematical framework is presented to characterize the radiative near-field distance in directional mmWave and THz communication systems under various realistic array rotations and misalignments. With the use of the developed framework, compact closed-form expressions are derived for the near-field boundary distance in a wide range of antenna configurations, including array-to-array and array-to-point setups, considering both linear and planar arrays. Our numerical study reveals that the presence of antenna misalignment may significantly adjust the boundaries of the near-field region in mmWave and THz communication systems.

</details>

### [18] [Design-MLLM: A Reinforcement Alignment Framework for Verifiable and Aesthetic Interior Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13312)
*Yuxuan Yang,Xiaotong Mao,Jingyao Wang,Fuchun Sun*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 论文提出Design-MLLM框架，通过强化学习对齐机制解决多模态大语言模型在室内设计中产生的空间可行性不足和美学偏好不一致问题，确保设计既满足硬约束又符合软偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs在室内设计应用中存在矛盾：虽然能解释用户意图并生成设计原理，但产生的布局往往不可建造且美学不一致。仅添加领域文本不足以解决问题，需要一种对齐机制来分离硬约束和软偏好并在优化中协调它们。

Method: 提出Design-MLLM强化学习对齐框架，采用可行性优先的偏好优化目标，通过双分支美学导向奖励实现：(1) 使用程序化约束检查显式评估空间可行性；(2) 仅在可行候选方案中评估美学偏好，避免视觉吸引但不可执行的捷径；(3) 执行组相对优化以获得稳定的偏好信号。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的广泛实验表明Design-MLLM的优势，学习到的可控策略能一致地选择和生成既可执行又美学一致的设计方案，而非偶尔产生视觉吸引但不可行的设计。

Conclusion: Design-MLLM通过强化学习对齐机制有效解决了MLLMs在室内设计中的可行性-美学矛盾，实现了硬约束与软偏好的协调优化，为实际部署提供了可靠的设计生成方案。

Abstract: Interior design is a requirements-to-visual-plan generation process that must simultaneously satisfy verifiable spatial feasibility and comparative aesthetic preferences. While recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs) offer a unified foundation for interpreting user intent and producing design rationales, our empirical analysis reveals a persistent contradiction in real-world deployment: MLLMs often produce layouts that are unbuildable and aesthetically inconsistent. These findings indicate that simply adding in-domain text is insufficient; effective interior design requires an alignment mechanism that separates hard constraints from soft preferences and coordinates them during optimization. To address this, we propose Design-MLLM, a reinforcement alignment framework that optimizes a feasibility-first preference objective via a dual-branch, aesthetic-oriented reward. Specifically, Design-MLLM (i) explicitly evaluates spatial feasibility using programmatic constraint checks, (ii) assesses aesthetic preference only among feasible candidates to avoid visually appealing but unexecutable shortcuts, and (iii) performs group-relative optimization to obtain stable preference signals. Through this process, Design-MLLM learns a controllable policy that consistently selects and generates solutions that are both executable and aesthetically coherent, rather than occasionally producing visually appealing but infeasible designs. Extensive experiments on various benchmark datasets demonstrate the advantages of Design-MLLM.

</details>


### [19] [Anchoring Emotions in Text: Robust Multimodal Fusion for Mimicry Intensity Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14976)
*Lingsi Zhu,Yuefeng Zou,Yunxiang Zhang,Naixiang Zheng,Guoyuan Wang,Jun Yu,Jiaen Liang,Wei Huang,Shengping Liu,Ximin Zheng*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: TAEMI是一个用于自然场景下情感模仿强度估计的多模态框架，通过文本锚定机制解决异构模态的非线性时序动态建模问题，并在不完美条件下保持鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然环境中估计情感模仿强度的主要挑战在于有效建模高度异构模态间的复杂非线性时序动态，特别是当物理信号被破坏或缺失时。连续视觉和声学信号容易受到瞬时环境噪声影响，而文本转录本则提供了稳定、时间无关的语义先验。

Method: 提出TAEMI框架，打破传统对称融合范式，利用文本转录本作为中心锚点。引入文本锚定双重交叉注意力机制，使用稳健的文本查询主动过滤帧级冗余并对齐噪声物理流。集成可学习的缺失模态令牌和模态丢弃策略，防止无约束真实场景中不可避免的缺失数据导致的性能灾难性下降。

Result: 在Hume-Vidmimic2数据集上的广泛实验表明，TAEMI能有效捕捉细粒度情感变化，并在不完美条件下保持稳健的预测弹性。该框架在六个连续情感维度上实现了最先进的平均皮尔逊相关系数，显著优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: TAEMI通过文本锚定方法成功解决了情感模仿强度估计中的多模态融合挑战，特别是在信号受损或缺失的情况下，为自然场景下的情感计算提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Estimating Emotional Mimicry Intensity (EMI) in naturalistic environments is a critical yet challenging task in affective computing. The primary difficulty lies in effectively modeling the complex, nonlinear temporal dynamics across highly heterogeneous modalities, especially when physical signals are corrupted or missing. To tackle this, we propose TAEMI (Text-Anchored Emotional Mimicry Intensity estimation), a novel multimodal framework designed for the 10th ABAW Competition. Motivated by the observation that continuous visual and acoustic signals are highly susceptible to transient environmental noise, we break the traditional symmetric fusion paradigm. Instead, we leverage textual transcript--which inherently encode a stable, time-independent semantic prior--as central anchors. Specifically, we introduce a Text-Anchored Dual Cross-Attention mechanism that utilizes these robust textual queries to actively filter out frame-level redundancies and align the noisy physical streams. Furthermore, to prevent catastrophic performance degradation caused by inevitably missing data in unconstrained real-world scenarios, we integrate Learnable Missing-Modality Tokens and a Modality Dropout strategy during training. Extensive experiments on the Hume-Vidmimic2 dataset demonstrate that TAEMI effectively captures fine-grained emotional variations and maintains robust predictive resilience under imperfect conditions. Our framework achieves a state-of-the-art mean Pearson correlation coefficient across six continuous emotional dimensions, significantly outperforming existing baseline methods.

</details>


### [20] [Multimodal Cyber-physical Interaction in XR: Hybrid Doctoral Thesis Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15392)
*Ahmad Alhilal,Kit Yung Lam,Lik-Hang Lee,Xuetong Wang,Sijia Li,Matti Siekkinen,Tristan Braud,Pan Hui*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 提出多模态框架支持从现场到VR/浏览器的混合参与模式，应用于首个混合式博士论文答辩，实现全身运动追踪和跨平台访问


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统学术活动局限于物理共处或平面视频会议，导致参与形式僵化和存在感碎片化，需要打破这种二元限制

Method: 开发多模态框架，集成全身运动追踪同步用户虚拟化身动作和手势，利用WebXR实现跨平台即时访问，支持从现场到VR/浏览器的混合参与

Result: 成功组织首个使用扩展现实(XR)的混合式博士论文答辩，用户反馈显示积极的VR体验，框架有效支持各种混合活动

Conclusion: 该框架打破了学术活动的二元参与限制，通过全身追踪和WebXR技术实现了自然交互和便捷访问，为混合学术活动提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: Academic events, such as a doctoral thesis defense, are typically limited to either physical co-location or flat video conferencing, resulting in rigid participation formats and fragmented presence. We present a multimodal framework that breaks this binary by supporting a spectrum of participation - from in-person attendance to immersive virtual reality (VR) or browser access - and report our findings from using it to organize the first ever hybrid doctoral thesis defense using extended reality (XR). The framework integrates full-body motion tracking to synchronize the user's avatar motions and gestures, enabling natural interaction with onsite participants as well as body language and gestures with remote attendees in the virtual world. It leverages WebXR to provide cross-platform and instant accessibility with easy setup. User feedback analysis reveals positive VR experiences and demonstrates the framework's effectiveness in supporting various hybrid event activities.

</details>

### [21] [Not All Prefills Are Equal: PPD Disaggregation for Multi-turn LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13358)
*Zongze Li,Jingyu Liu,Zach Xu,Yineng Zhang,Tahseen Rabbani,Ce Zhang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: PPD是一种动态路由系统，通过将追加预填充请求路由到解码节点，减少多轮对话中的KV传输和延迟


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Prefill-Decode架构在多轮对话中存在两个基本低效问题：每轮都需要重新预填充整个上下文，以及重复的KV传输导致带宽饱和和高延迟

Method: 提出PPD（Prefill Prefill-capable Decode）解耦架构，动态路由系统决定何时在解码节点本地处理第二轮及以后的请求，利用缓存的KV状态，避免不必要的KV传输

Result: PPD将第二轮及以后的首次令牌时间减少68%，同时保持竞争力的每输出令牌时间，有效缓解高负载下的KV传输拥塞

Conclusion: PPD代表了一种灵活高效的多轮LLM服务范式，能够适应不同的服务级别目标，并与传统PD部署无缝集成

Abstract: Prefill-Decode (PD) disaggregation has become the standard architecture for modern LLM inference engines, which alleviates the interference of two distinctive workloads. With the growing demand for multi-turn interactions in chatbots and agentic systems, we re-examined PD in this case and found two fundamental inefficiencies: (1) every turn requires prefilling the new prompt and response from the last turn, and (2) repeated KV transfers between prefill and decode nodes saturate the bandwidth, leading to high latency and even service degradation. Our key insight is that not all prefill operations are equally disruptive: append-prefill -- processing only the new input tokens while reusing cached KV states -- incurs substantially less decoding slowdown than full prefill. This motivates routing append-prefill to decode nodes locally. However, through comprehensive analysis, we show that no single fixed routing strategy satisfies all Service Level Objectives (SLOs) simultaneously. Based on this insight, we propose Prefill Prefill-capable Decode (PPD) disaggregation, a dynamic routing system that decides when to process Turn 2+ requests locally on decode nodes using cached KV states. PPD adapts to varying SLOs via configurable weights and seamlessly integrates with traditional PD deployments. With extensive evaluations, we show that PPD reduces Turn 2+ time-to-first-token (TTFT) by 68% while maintaining competitive time-per-output-token (TPOT), effectively alleviating KV transfer congestion under high load. We believe PPD represents a flexible and efficient paradigm for multi-turn LLM serving.

</details>


### [22] [A Case for CATS: A Conductor-driven Asymmetric Transport Scheme for Semantic Prioritization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13945)
*Syed Muhammad Aqdas Rizvi*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: CATS框架为TCP引入语义感知能力，通过集中式调度器优先传输关键内容，显著改善延迟敏感应用的性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统TCP作为盲目的FIFO数据传输模型，无法满足延迟敏感和交互式应用的需求。HTTP/1.1的并行连接开销高，HTTP/2存在传输层队头阻塞问题，HTTP/3在QUIC上的优先级实现存在异构性问题，这些历史性能解决方案都存在局限性

Method: 提出CATS（Conductor-driven Asymmetric Transport Scheme）框架，在传输层引入"Conductor"集中式调度器，为TCP提供语义感知能力，基于TCP BBR实现，优先传输关键内容

Result: 在ns-3模拟中，代表性网页下载的最坏情况下，First Contentful Paint减少超过78%，总页面加载时间相比基线无损失

Conclusion: CATS通过为TCP引入语义感知和集中式调度，有效解决了传统传输协议的局限性，显著提升了用户感知性能，为延迟敏感应用提供了更优的传输方案

Abstract: Standard transport protocols like TCP operate as a blind, FIFO conveyor belt for data, a model that is increasingly suboptimal for latency-sensitive and interactive applications. This paper challenges this model by introducing CATS (Conductor-driven Asymmetric Transport Scheme), a framework that provides TCP with the semantic awareness necessary to prioritize critical content. By centralizing scheduling intelligence in a transport-native "Conductor", CATS significantly improves user-perceived performance by delivering essential data first. This architecture directly confronts a cascade of historical performance workarounds and their limitations, including the high overhead of parallel connections in HTTP/1.1, the transport-layer Head-of-Line blocking in HTTP/2, and the observed implementation heterogeneity of prioritization in HTTP/3 over QUIC. Built upon TCP BBR, our ns-3 implementation demonstrates this principle by reducing the First Contentful Paint by over 78% in a representative webpage download configured as a deliberate worst-case scenario, with no penalty to total page load time compared to the baseline.

</details>


### [23] [AtlasRAN: Modeling and Performance Evaluation of Open 5G Platforms for Ubiquitous Wireless Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14661)
*Ryan Barker,Tolunay Seyfi,Alireza Ebrahimi Dorcheh,Julia Boone,Fatemeh Afghah,Joseph Boccuzzi*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: AtlasRAN是一个面向能力的框架，用于建模和评估5G O-RAN平台，强调不同评估环境（模拟器、仿真器、数字孪生、硬件在环）在时序、I/O和控制循环行为上的差异，并提供参考架构、术语和能力矩阵来指导可信的研究评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前5G系统作为车联网、路边边缘平台和未来无人系统应用的共享基础设施，但不同评估环境（模拟器、仿真器、数字孪生、硬件在环）的时序、I/O和控制循环行为存在差异，导致性能评估结果可能反映的是执行框架的限制而非无线设计本身的问题。

Method: 提出AtlasRAN框架，包含：1）两种参考架构；2）区分功能兼容性和时序保真度的术语；3）将研究问题映射到可信评估环境的能力矩阵。使用O-RAN作为实验坐标系，涵盖CU/DU分区、前传传输、控制暴露和核心网锚定。通过CPU-GPU边缘平台上的CU-DU上行链路负载研究进行验证。

Result: 在CPU-GPU边缘平台的验证实验中，无论是CPU-only基线还是GPU加速的LDPC解码变体，当用户数从1增加到12时，聚合吞吐量急剧下降，而公平性保持接近理想，计算利用率反而降低。这表明扩展限制主要源于仿真框架中的时间尺度扩张和在线I/O饥饿，而非解码器饱和。

Conclusion: 评估无处不在的5G基础设施时，必须将时序、内存和传输语义作为首要实验变量进行报告。不同评估环境在时序、I/O和控制循环行为上的差异可能导致性能评估偏差，因此需要系统化的框架来确保评估的可信度。

Abstract: Fifth-generation (5G) systems are increasingly studied as shared communication and computing infrastructure for connected vehicles, roadside edge platforms, and future unmanned-system applications. Yet results from simulators, host-OS emulators, digital twins, and hardware-in-the-loop testbeds are often compared as if timing, input/output (I/O), and control-loop behavior were equivalent across them. They are not. Consequently, apparent limits in throughput, latency, scalability, or real-time behavior may reflect the execution harness rather than the wireless design itself.
  This paper presents \textit{AtlasRAN}, a capability-oriented framework for modeling and performance evaluation of 5G Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN) platforms. It introduces two reference architectures, terminology that separates functional compatibility from timing fidelity, and a capability matrix that maps research questions to evaluation environments that can support them credibly. O-RAN is used here as an experimental coordinate system spanning Centralized Unit (CU)/Distributed Unit (DU) partitioning, fronthaul transport, control exposure, and core-network anchoring.
  We validate \textit{AtlasRAN} through a CU-DU uplink load study on a coherent CPU-GPU edge platform. For both a CPU-only baseline and a GPU-accelerated low-density parity-check decoding variant, aggregate goodput drops sharply as user count rises from 1 to 12, while fairness remains near ideal and compute utilization decreases rather than increases. This pattern indicates time-scale dilation and online I/O starvation in the emulation harness, not decoder saturation, as the dominant scaling limit. The key lesson is that timing, memory, and transport semantics must be reported as first-class experimental variables when evaluating ubiquitous 5G infrastructure.

</details>

### [24] [Translational Gaps in Graph Transformers for Longitudinal EHR Prediction: A Critical Appraisal of GT-BEHRT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13231)
*Krish Tadigotla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 对GT-BEHRT图-Transformer架构在电子健康记录分析中的批判性综述，指出其性能优势但存在评估方法学缺陷


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer模型将临床就诊视为无序代码集合，无法捕捉就诊内的有意义关系，需要图-Transformer方法在保留长期时序模式能力的同时建模就诊级结构

Method: 对GT-BEHRT架构进行七维度分析：表示设计、预训练策略、队列构建透明度、超越区分度的评估、公平性评估、可重复性、部署可行性

Result: GT-BEHRT在心衰预测上报告了强区分性能（AUROC 94.37±0.20，AUPRC 73.96±0.83，F1 64.70±0.85），但存在校准分析缺失、公平性评估不完整、对队列选择敏感、跨表型和预测时间范围分析有限、部署考虑不足等缺陷

Conclusion: GT-BEHRT代表了EHR表示学习的有意义架构进展，但在可靠支持临床决策前需要更严格的评估，重点关注校准、公平性和部署可行性

Abstract: Transformer-based models have improved predictive modeling on longitudinal electronic health records through large-scale self-supervised pretraining. However, most EHR transformer architectures treat each clinical encounter as an unordered collection of codes, which limits their ability to capture meaningful relationships within a visit. Graph-transformer approaches aim to address this limitation by modeling visit-level structure while retaining the ability to learn long-term temporal patterns. This paper provides a critical review of GT-BEHRT, a graph-transformer architecture evaluated on MIMIC-IV intensive care outcomes and heart failure prediction in the All of Us Research Program. We examine whether the reported performance gains reflect genuine architectural benefits and whether the evaluation methodology supports claims of robustness and clinical relevance. We analyze GT-BEHRT across seven dimensions relevant to modern machine learning systems, including representation design, pretraining strategy, cohort construction transparency, evaluation beyond discrimination, fairness assessment, reproducibility, and deployment feasibility. GT-BEHRT reports strong discrimination for heart failure prediction within 365 days, with AUROC 94.37 +/- 0.20, AUPRC 73.96 +/- 0.83, and F1 64.70 +/- 0.85. Despite these results, we identify several important gaps, including the lack of calibration analysis, incomplete fairness evaluation, sensitivity to cohort selection, limited analysis across phenotypes and prediction horizons, and limited discussion of practical deployment considerations. Overall, GT-BEHRT represents a meaningful architectural advance in EHR representation learning, but more rigorous evaluation focused on calibration, fairness, and deployment is needed before such models can reliably support clinical decision-making.

</details>


### [25] [RFX-Fuse: Breiman and Cutler's Unified ML Engine + Native Explainable Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13234)
*Chris Kuchar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RFX-Fuse实现了Breiman和Cutler原始随机森林的完整愿景，作为一个统一的机器学习引擎，提供分类、回归、无监督学习、相似性度量、异常检测、缺失值插补和可视化等功能，支持GPU/CPU加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习流程需要多个独立工具（如XGBoost、FAISS、SHAP、Isolation Forest等）来完成不同任务，而Breiman和Cutler的原始随机森林设计是一个统一引擎，包含多种功能，但现代库（如scikit-learn）从未实现这些完整功能。

Method: RFX-Fuse通过单一模型对象（一次生长的树集）实现完整功能，主要创新包括：(1) 邻近重要性——原生可解释相似性：邻近度衡量样本相似性，邻近重要性解释原因；(2) 数据集特定的插补验证——通过评估插补数据看起来有多真实来对插补方法进行排名，无需真实标签。

Result: RFX-Fuse提供了一个1-2个模型对象的替代方案，替代了现代ML流程中需要的5+个独立工具，实现了原始随机森林的完整功能集，并支持GPU/CPU加速。

Conclusion: RFX-Fuse实现了Breiman和Cutler对随机森林作为统一机器学习引擎的完整愿景，通过单一模型对象提供全面的功能，减少了现代ML流程中对多个独立工具的依赖，并引入了邻近重要性和数据集特定插补验证等创新功能。

Abstract: Breiman and Cutler's original Random Forest was designed as a unified ML engine -- not merely an ensemble predictor. Their implementation included classification, regression, unsupervised learning, proximity-based similarity, outlier
  detection, missing value imputation, and visualization -- capabilities that modern libraries like scikit-learn never implemented. RFX-Fuse (Random Forests X [X=compression] -- Forest Unified Learning and Similarity Engine) delivers
  Breiman and Cutler's complete vision with native GPU/CPU support. Modern ML pipelines require 5+ separate tools -- XGBoost for prediction, FAISS for similarity, SHAP for explanations, Isolation Forest for outliers, custom code for
  importance. RFX-Fuse provides a 1 to 2 model object alternative -- a single set of trees grown once. Novel Contributions: (1) Proximity Importance -- native explainable similarity: proximity measures that samples are similar;
  proximity importance explains why. (2) Dataset-specific imputation validation for general tabular data -- ranking imputation methods by how real the imputed data looks, without ground truth labels.

</details>


### [26] [Continual Fine-Tuning with Provably Accurate and Parameter-Free Task Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13235)
*Hang Thi-Thuy Le,Long Minh Bui,Minh Hoang,Trong Nghia Hoang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种结合输入适应和参数适应优势的持续微调方法，通过参数自由检索实现测试时自适应表示使用


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续微调方法存在局限性：输入适应方法需要持续学习检索函数且容易遗忘，参数适应方法使用固定输入嵌入函数避免了遗忘但牺牲了表示适应性。需要结合两者的优势。

Method: 提出基于聚类的参数自由检索范式，包含两个关键组件：(1)自适应模块组合策略，学习信息丰富的任务特定更新以保留和补充先验知识；(2)基于聚类的检索机制，捕获每个任务的独特表示特征，实现测试时自适应表示使用。

Result: 广泛实验表明，这些组件协同工作，在任务语义大幅变化的情况下显著提高了检索和预测性能。理论分析提供了任务检索误差界限，将低检索误差与任务特定表示簇的结构特性联系起来。

Conclusion: 该方法成功结合了输入适应和参数适应方法的优势，通过参数自由检索实现自适应表示使用，在任务语义大幅变化时仍能保持良好性能，为持续学习提供了新的理论见解和实践方案。

Abstract: Continual fine-tuning aims to adapt a pre-trained backbone to new tasks sequentially while preserving performance on earlier tasks whose data are no longer available. Existing approaches fall into two categories which include input- and parameter-adaptation. Input-adaptation methods rely on retrieving the most relevant prompts at test time, but require continuously learning a retrieval function that is prone to forgetting. Parameter-adaptation methods instead use a fixed input embedding function to enable retrieval-free prediction and avoid forgetting, but sacrifice representation adaptability. To combine their best strengths, we propose a new parameter-adaptation method that enables adaptive use of input embeddings during test time with parameter-free retrieval. We derive task-retrieval error bounds for a clustering-based, parameter-free paradigm, providing theoretical guarantees that link low retrieval error to structural properties of task-specific representation clusters, revealing a fresh insight into how well-organized clustering structure will enable reliable retrieval. Motivated by this insight, our method is designed with two key components: (i) an adaptive module composition strategy that learns informative task-specific updates to preserve and complement prior knowledge, and (ii) a clustering-based retrieval mechanism that captures distinct representation signatures for each task, enabling adaptive representation use at test time. Extensive experiments show that these components work synergistically to improve retrieval and predictive performance under large shifts in task semantics.

</details>


### [27] [Introducing Feature-Based Trajectory Clustering, a clustering algorithm for longitudinal data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13254)
*Marie-Pierre Sylvestre,Laurence Boulanger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种新的纵向数据聚类算法，通过数学公式将个体映射到欧几里得空间，然后使用谱聚类识别具有相似时间演化特征的个体群组


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向数据中个体的时间依赖性变量演化方式各不相同，但可能存在许多个体共享的共性特征。需要一种方法能够识别出具有相似时间演化特征的个体群组。

Method: 采用两步法：第一步通过特定数学公式将每个个体映射到欧几里得空间中的点，这些公式旨在捕捉各种特征性时间演化模式；第二步对生成的点云应用谱聚类算法来识别聚类。

Result: 提出了一种新的纵向数据聚类框架，能够有效识别具有相似时间演化特征的个体群组，为纵向数据分析提供了新的聚类方法。

Conclusion: 该方法通过将纵向数据转化为欧几里得空间表示，结合谱聚类算法，能够有效发现个体间共享的时间演化特征模式，为纵向数据分析提供了实用的聚类工具。

Abstract: We present a new algorithm for clustering longitudinal data. Data of this type can be conceptualized as consisting of individuals and, for each such individual, observations of a time-dependent variable made at various times. Generically, the specific way in which this variable evolves with time is different from one individual to the next. However, there may also be commonalities; specific characteristic features of the time evolution shared by many individuals. The purpose of the method we put forward is to find clusters of individual whose underlying time-dependent variables share such characteristic features. This is done in two steps. The first step identifies each individual to a point in Euclidean space whose coordinates are determined by specific mathematical formulae meant to capture a variety of characteristic features. The second step finds the clusters by applying the Spectral Clustering algorithm to the resulting point cloud.

</details>


### [28] [Domain-Skewed Federated Learning with Feature Decoupling and Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14238)
*Huan Wang,Jun Shen,Jun Yan,Guansong Pang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出F²DC方法解决联邦学习中的域偏移问题，通过特征解耦和校准提升多域泛化能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中域偏移导致客户端数据来自不同领域，阻碍全局模型学习一致的表示空间，影响多域泛化能力

Method: 提出F²DC方法，包含域特征解耦器(DFD)分离域鲁棒特征和域相关特征，域特征校正器(DFC)校准域相关特征以捕获额外类别相关信息，最后进行域感知聚合

Result: 在三个流行的多域数据集上验证了F²DC的有效性，证明了其两个模块的贡献

Conclusion: 通过特征解耦和校准解决域偏移问题，提升联邦学习在多域场景下的表示一致性和泛化能力

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) allows distributed clients to collaboratively train a global model in a privacy-preserving manner. However, one major challenge is domain skew, where clients' data originating from diverse domains may hinder the aggregated global model from learning a consistent representation space, resulting in poor generalizable ability in multiple domains. In this paper, we argue that the domain skew is reflected in the domain-specific biased features of each client, causing the local model's representations to collapse into a narrow low-dimensional subspace. We then propose Federated Feature Decoupling and Calibration ($F^2$DC), which liberates valuable class-relevant information by calibrating the domain-specific biased features, enabling more consistent representations across domains. A novel component, Domain Feature Decoupler (DFD), is first introduced in $F^2$DC to determine the robustness of each feature unit, thereby separating the local features into domain-robust features and domain-related features. A Domain Feature Corrector (DFC) is further proposed to calibrate these domain-related features by explicitly linking discriminative signals, capturing additional class-relevant clues that complement the domain-robust features. Finally, a domain-aware aggregation of the local models is performed to promote consensus among clients. Empirical results on three popular multi-domain datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed $F^2$DC and the contributions of its two modules. Code is available at https://github.com/mala-lab/F2DC.

</details>


### [29] [Beyond Attention: True Adaptive World Models via Spherical Kernel Operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13263)
*Vladimer Khasia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出球形核算子(SKO)框架替代标准注意力机制，通过将数据流形投影到统一超球面并使用超球多项式进行积分重构，克服传统世界模型构建中的数学缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于参数化潜在空间的世界模型构建存在数学缺陷：仅将流形学习问题转移到潜在空间，当数据分布变化时潜在流形随之变化，迫使预测算子隐式重新学习新的拓扑结构。此外，点积注意力等正算子存在饱和现象，永久限制其预测能力并使其易受维度诅咒影响。

Method: 引入球形核算子(SKO)框架替代标准注意力机制：1) 将未知数据流形投影到统一的超球面环境；2) 使用局部化的超球(Gegenbauer)多项式序列；3) 执行目标函数的直接积分重构。该框架将未归一化输出形式化为真实测度支持估计器，从数学上将真实环境转移动态与智能体的有偏观测频率解耦。

Result: SKO避免了饱和现象，其近似误差界限严格依赖于内在流形维度q而非环境维度。实证评估证实SKO显著加速收敛，在自回归语言建模中优于标准注意力基线。

Conclusion: SKO为世界模型构建提供了数学严谨的范式，通过重新定义核心预测机制，解决了传统方法的根本数学缺陷，实现了更好的理论保证和实证性能。

Abstract: The pursuit of world model based artificial intelligence has predominantly relied on projecting high-dimensional observations into parameterized latent spaces, wherein transition dynamics are subsequently learned. However, this conventional paradigm is mathematically flawed: it merely displaces the manifold learning problem into the latent space. When the underlying data distribution shifts, the latent manifold shifts accordingly, forcing the predictive operator to implicitly relearn the new topological structure. Furthermore, by classical approximation theory, positive operators like dot product attention inevitably suffer from the saturation phenomenon, permanently bottlenecking their predictive capacity and leaving them vulnerable to the curse of dimensionality. In this paper, we formulate a mathematically rigorous paradigm for world model construction by redefining the core predictive mechanism. Inspired by Ryan O'Dowd's foundational work we introduce Spherical Kernel Operator (SKO), a framework that replaces standard attention. By projecting the unknown data manifold onto a unified ambient hypersphere and utilizing a localized sequence of ultraspherical (Gegenbauer) polynomials, SKO performs direct integral reconstruction of the target function. Because this localized spherical polynomial kernel is not strictly positive, it bypasses the saturation phenomenon, yielding approximation error bounds that depend strictly on the intrinsic manifold dimension q, rather than the ambient dimension. Furthermore, by formalizing its unnormalized output as an authentic measure support estimator, SKO mathematically decouples the true environmental transition dynamics from the biased observation frequency of the agent. Empirical evaluations confirm that SKO significantly accelerates convergence and outperforms standard attention baselines in autoregressive language modeling.

</details>


### [30] [Federated Personal Knowledge Graph Completion with Lightweight Large Language Models for Personalized Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13264)
*Fernando Spadea,Oshani Seneviratne*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedTREK-LM是一个结合轻量级大语言模型、演化个人知识图谱、联邦学习和Kahneman-Tversky优化的联邦推荐框架，在去中心化个性化推荐中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着个性化推荐越来越依赖用户隐私数据，需要能够在不需要集中用户信息的情况下适应个体用户的解决方案。现有的推荐方法在保护隐私和实现有效个性化之间存在矛盾。

Method: FedTREK-LM框架统一了四个关键组件：1) 轻量级大语言模型（Qwen3系列），2) 演化个人知识图谱，3) 联邦学习实现去中心化训练，4) Kahneman-Tversky优化方法。通过使用结构化PKG提示LLM，实现上下文感知的个性化推荐推理。

Result: 在三个轻量级Qwen3模型（0.6B、1.7B、4B）上，FedTREK-LM在电影和食谱推荐基准测试中持续显著优于最先进的KG补全和联邦推荐基线方法（HAKE、KBGAT、FedKGRec），F1分数提升超过4倍。真实用户数据对有效个性化至关重要，合成数据会使性能下降高达46%。

Conclusion: FedTREK-LM为自适应、LLM驱动的个性化推荐提供了一个实用范式，能够泛化到去中心化、演化的用户PKG场景，在保护隐私的同时实现有效的个性化推荐。

Abstract: Personalized recommendation increasingly relies on private user data, motivating approaches that can adapt to individuals without centralizing their information. We present Federated Targeted Recommendations with Evolving Knowledge graphs and Language Models (FedTREK-LM), a framework that unifies lightweight large language models (LLMs), evolving personal knowledge graphs (PKGs), federated learning (FL), and Kahneman-Tversky Optimization to enable scalable, decentralized personalization. By prompting LLMs with structured PKGs, FedTREK-LM performs context-aware reasoning for personalized recommendation tasks such as movie and recipe suggestions. Across three lightweight Qwen3 models (0.6B, 1.7B, 4B), FedTREK-LM consistently and substantially outperforms state-of-the-art KG completion and federated recommendation baselines (HAKE, KBGAT, and FedKGRec), achieving more than a 4x improvement in F1-score on the movie and food benchmarks. Our results further show that real user data is critical for effective personalization, as synthetic data degrades performance by up to 46%. Overall, FedTREK-LM offers a practical paradigm for adaptive, LLM-powered personalization that generalizes across decentralized, evolving user PKGs.

</details>


### [31] [Knowledge, Rules and Their Embeddings: Two Paths towards Neuro-Symbolic JEPA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13265)
*Yongchao Huang,Hassan Raza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RiJEPA框架，通过能量约束和多模态双编码器将结构化归纳偏置注入JEPA训练，将离散符号规则松弛为连续可微逻辑，实现双向神经符号表示学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代自监督预测架构擅长捕捉高维数据中的复杂统计相关性，但缺乏内化可验证人类逻辑的机制，容易受到虚假相关性和捷径学习的影响。而传统的基于规则的推理系统虽然提供严谨、可解释的逻辑，但存在离散边界和NP难组合爆炸问题。需要弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: 提出基于规则信息的联合嵌入预测架构(RiJEPA)的双向神经符号框架。第一方向：通过能量约束(EBC)和多模态双编码器架构将结构化归纳偏置注入JEPA训练，重塑表示流形。第二方向：将刚性离散符号规则松弛为连续可微逻辑，利用梯度引导的朗之万扩散在规则能量景观中进行连续规则发现，实现无条件联合生成、条件前向和溯因推理以及边缘预测翻译。

Result: 在合成拓扑模拟和高风险临床用例上的实证评估证实了该方法的有效性。该框架为鲁棒、生成式和可解释的神经符号表示学习建立了强大基础。

Conclusion: RiJEPA框架成功弥合了统计学习与符号推理之间的鸿沟，通过双向神经符号方法实现了既具有统计能力又具备逻辑严谨性的表示学习，为可解释AI提供了新范式。

Abstract: Modern self-supervised predictive architectures excel at capturing complex statistical correlations from high-dimensional data but lack mechanisms to internalize verifiable human logic, leaving them susceptible to spurious correlations and shortcut learning. Conversely, traditional rule-based inference systems offer rigorous, interpretable logic but suffer from discrete boundaries and NP-hard combinatorial explosion. To bridge this divide, we propose a bidirectional neuro-symbolic framework centered around Rule-informed Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (RiJEPA). In the first direction, we inject structured inductive biases into JEPA training via Energy-Based Constraints (EBC) and a multi-modal dual-encoder architecture. This fundamentally reshapes the representation manifold, replacing arbitrary statistical correlations with geometrically sound logical basins. In the second direction, we demonstrate that by relaxing rigid, discrete symbolic rules into a continuous, differentiable logic, we can bypass traditional combinatorial search for new rule generation. By leveraging gradient-guided Langevin diffusion within the rule energy landscape, we introduce novel paradigms for continuous rule discovery, which enable unconditional joint generation, conditional forward and abductive inference, and marginal predictive translation. Empirical evaluations on both synthetic topological simulations and a high-stakes clinical use case confirm the efficacy of our approach. Ultimately, this framework establishes a powerful foundation for robust, generative, and interpretable neuro-symbolic representation learning.

</details>


### [32] [CAMEL-CLIP: Channel-aware Multimodal Electroencephalography-text Alignment for Generalizable Brain Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13272)
*Hanseul Choi,Jinyeong Park,Seongwon Jin,Sungho Park,Jibum Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CAMEL-CLIP是一种面向EEG的对比学习多模态基础模型，通过通道感知设计解决EEG通道异质性问题，在多种下游任务中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG基础模型对通道异质性（如通道组成或顺序变化）敏感，限制了其泛化能力和实际应用。

Method: 提出CAMEL-CLIP模型，包含三个核心组件：1)基于通道属性的位置编码，通过语义信息识别通道；2)动态通道投影，独立投影每个通道而不压缩特征；3)双级对比学习，联合执行通道级和样本级对比学习。

Result: 实验结果表明，CAMEL-CLIP在线性探测下达到最先进性能，并优于依赖完全微调的现有基础模型。

Conclusion: CAMEL-CLIP通过通道感知设计有效解决了EEG通道异质性问题，为EEG基础模型提供了更鲁棒和通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) foundation models have shown promise for learning generalizable representations, yet they remain sensitive to channel heterogeneity, such as changes in channel composition or ordering. We propose channel-aware multimodal EEG-text alignment contrastive language-image pretraining (CAMEL-CLIP), a contrastive EEG-text multimodal foundation model designed to be robust to heterogeneous channel configurations and widely applicable to diverse downstream tasks. CAMEL-CLIP introduces three key components: (1) channel attribute-based positional encoding, which identifies channels through semantic information; (2) dynamic channel projection, which generates variable-length embeddings by independently projecting each channel without feature compression; and (3) dual-level contrastive learning, which jointly performs channel-level and sample-level contrastive learning to capture both channel-specific and global signal characteristics. Experimental results demonstrate that CAMEL-CLIP achieves state-of-the-art performance under linear-probing and outperforms existing foundation models that rely on full-finetuning.

</details>


### [33] [Joint Routing and Model Pruning for Decentralized Federated Learning in Bandwidth-Constrained Multi-Hop Wireless Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15188)
*Xiaoyu He,Weicai Li,Tiejun Lv,Xi Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出联合路由与剪枝框架优化去中心化联邦学习的通信效率，通过路径选择和参数保留率优化在通信约束下提升收敛性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化联邦学习（D-FL）在多跳模型交换和聚合过程中常受通信资源限制的瓶颈，需要解决通信延迟约束下的模型传输效率问题

Method: 提出联合路由与剪枝框架，分析模型偏差总和对收敛界的影响，将问题转化为路由优化，开发延迟高效的传输路径选择算法

Result: 相比未剪枝系统，平均传输延迟降低27.8%，测试准确率提升约12%；相比基准路由算法，准确率提升约8%

Conclusion: 通过优化路由路径和剪枝率，在通信约束下最大化模型保留率，有效降低模型偏差，显著提升去中心化联邦学习的收敛性能

Abstract: Decentralized federated learning (D-FL) enables privacy-preserving training without a central server, but multi-hop model exchanges and aggregation are often bottlenecked by communication resource constraints. To address this issue, we propose a joint routing-and-pruning framework that optimizes routing paths and pruning rates to maintain communication latency within prescribed limits. We analyze how the sum of model biases across all clients affects the convergence bound of D-FL and formulate an optimization problem that maximizes the model retention rate to minimize these biases under communication constraints. Further analysis reveals that each client's model retention rate is path-dependent, which reduces the original problem to a routing optimization. Leveraging this insight, we develop a routing algorithm that selects latency-efficient transmission paths, allowing more parameters to be delivered within the time budget and thereby improving D-FL convergence. Simulations demonstrate that, compared with unpruned systems, the proposed framework reduces average transmission latency by 27.8% and improves testing accuracy by approximately 12%. Furthermore, relative to standard benchmark routing algorithms, the proposed routing method improves accuracy by roughly 8%.

</details>


### [34] [Learning from Partial Chain-of-Thought via Truncated-Reasoning Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13274)
*Gianluigi Silvestri,Edoardo Cetin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRSD是一种轻量级后训练方法，通过自蒸馏使模型能够从部分推理轨迹中产生正确预测，减少推理计算成本


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向推理的语言模型在推理时生成长思维链会带来大量计算成本，这些成本往往体现在冗余或低效的推理过程中

Method: TRSD（截断推理自蒸馏）：冻结教师模型生成完整推理轨迹并评估对应答案分布，构建合成训练目标；相同架构的学生模型仅基于推理轨迹的截断前缀进行训练以匹配教师答案分布

Result: TRSD提高了截断推理的鲁棒性，在多种推理基准和token预算下显著减少了准确率损失；TRSD训练模型即使没有截断干预也能输出更短的推理轨迹，显著降低推理时成本

Conclusion: TRSD是一种有效的轻量级后训练方法，能够在不牺牲准确性的前提下显著降低推理计算成本，提高推理效率

Abstract: Reasoning-oriented language models achieve strong performance by generating long chain-of-thought traces at inference time. However, this capability comes with substantial and often excessive computational cost, which can materialize in redundant or inefficient reasoning. We study this setting and introduce Truncated-Reasoning Self-Distillation (TRSD), a lightweight post-training procedure that encourages models to produce correct predictions from partial reasoning traces. In TRSD, a frozen teacher model first generates a full reasoning trace and evaluates the corresponding answer distribution conditioned on the prompt and the complete reasoning to construct a synthetic training target. A student model with the same architecture is then trained to match the teacher's answer distribution while being conditioned only on a truncated prefix of its reasoning trace. Across multiple reasoning benchmarks and token budgets, we demonstrate that TRSD improves robustness to truncated inference, with far reduced accuracy tradeoffs when applied to a diverse set of reasoning models. Moreover, although never explicitly regularized for shorter generation during training, we also find that TRSD-trained models inherently output shorter reasoning traces without truncation, significantly reducing inference-time costs even without artificial interventions.

</details>


### [35] [PREBA: Surgical Duration Prediction via PCA-Weighted Retrieval-Augmented LLMs and Bayesian Averaging Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13275)
*Wanyin Wu,Kanxue Li,Baosheng Yu,Haoyun Zhao,Yibing Zhan,Dapeng Tao,Hua Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PREBA是一个检索增强框架，通过PCA加权检索和贝叶斯平均聚合，将LLM预测基于机构特定的临床证据和统计先验，显著提升手术时长预测准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术时长预测方法存在局限：监督学习方法需要高质量标注数据和计算密集型训练；零样本LLM推理缺乏机构特定的临床上下文（如本地人口统计学和病例组合分布），导致预测临床不匹配且不稳定。需要一种无需训练、能融入机构特定临床证据的方法。

Method: PREBA框架包含三个核心组件：1) 将异构临床特征编码到统一表示空间实现系统检索；2) PCA加权检索识别临床相关历史病例作为LLM的证据上下文；3) 贝叶斯平均聚合将多轮LLM预测与群体级统计先验融合，产生校准的临床合理估计。

Result: 在两个真实临床数据集上使用三种SOTA LLMs（Qwen3、DeepSeek-R1、HuatuoGPT-o1）评估。PREBA显著提升性能：MAE降低达40%，R²从-0.13提升至0.62（相比零样本推理），达到与监督ML方法竞争的准确性，展示了强有效性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: PREBA通过检索增强框架成功解决了零样本LLM推理在手术时长预测中的临床不匹配问题，无需训练即可融入机构特定临床证据，在保持计算效率的同时达到与监督方法相当的准确性，为医院资源管理提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of surgical duration is pivotal for hospital resource management. Although recent supervised learning approaches-from machine learning (ML) to fine-tuned large language models (LLMs)-have shown strong performance, they remain constrained by the need for high-quality labeled data and computationally intensive training. In contrast, zero-shot LLM inference offers a promising training-free alternative but it lacks grounding in institution-specific clinical context (e.g., local demographics and case-mix distributions), making its predictions clinically misaligned and prone to instability. To address these limitations, we present PREBA, a retrieval-augmented framework that integrates PCA-weighted retrieval and Bayesian averaging aggregation to ground LLM predictions in institution-specific clinical evidence and statistical priors. The core of PREBA is to construct an evidence-based prompt for the LLM, comprising (1) the most clinically similar historical surgical cases and (2) clinical statistical priors. To achieve this, PREBA first encodes heterogeneous clinical features into a unified representation space enabling systematic retrieval. It then performs PCA-weighted retrieval to identify clinically relevant historical cases, which form the evidence context supplied to the LLM. Finally, PREBA applies Bayesian averaging to fuse multi-round LLM predictions with population-level statistical priors, yielding calibrated and clinically plausible duration estimates. We evaluate PREBA on two real-world clinical datasets using three state-of-the-art LLMs, including Qwen3, DeepSeek-R1, and HuatuoGPT-o1. PREBA significantly improves performance-for instance, reducing MAE by up to 40% and raising R^2 from -0.13 to 0.62 over zero-shot inference-and it achieves accuracy competitive with supervised ML methods, demonstrating strong effectiveness and generalization.

</details>


### [36] [FastODT: A tree-based framework for efficient continual learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13276)
*Daniel Bretsko,Piotr Walas,Devashish Khulbe,Sebastian Stros,Stanislav Sobolevsky,Tomas Satura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Hoeffding界限控制生长的遗忘树模型，用于非平稳环境下的在线学习，在能源和环境感知时间序列基准测试中表现优异，计算效率高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的机器学习模型需要在数据分布不断演变且计算资源受限的环境中运行，特别是在能源时间序列、天气监测和环境感知等非平稳领域。模型需要具备适应性、持续学习和长期知识保留能力。

Method: 引入基于Hoeffding界限控制生长的遗忘树模型，无缝集成快速学习和推理与高效内存管理及鲁棒知识保留，支持在线学习。

Result: 在能源和环境感知时间序列基准测试中，该框架的性能与现有在线和批量学习方法相当甚至超越，同时保持卓越的计算效率。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了适应性、持续更新和高效重训练的核心目标，无需完整模型重训练，为资源受限且需要持续适应的现实世界非平稳环境提供了可扩展且资源感知的基础。

Abstract: Machine learning models deployed in real-world settings must operate under evolving data distributions and constrained computational resources. This challenge is particularly acute in non-stationary domains such as energy time series, weather monitoring, and environmental sensing. To remain effective, models must support adaptability, continuous learning, and long-term knowledge retention. This paper introduces a oblivious tree-based model with Hoeffding bound controlling its growth. It seamlessly integrates rapid learning and inference with efficient memory management and robust knowledge preservation, thus allowing for online learning. Extensive experiments across energy and environmental sensing time-series benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves performance competitive with, and in several cases surpassing, existing online and batch learning methods, while maintaining superior computational efficiency. Collectively, these results demonstrate that the proposed approach fulfills the core objectives of adaptability, continual updating, and efficient retraining without full model retraining. The framework provides a scalable and resource-aware foundation for deployment in real-world non-stationary environments where resources are constrained and sustained adaptation is essential.

</details>


### [37] [Learning Retrieval Models with Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13277)
*Thibault Formal,Maxime Louis,Hervé Dejean,Stéphane Clinchant*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPLARE是一种基于稀疏自编码器的学习稀疏检索方法，在多语言和跨域检索任务中优于基于词汇表的传统方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏自编码器(SAEs)能够将大语言模型的密集表示分解为可解释的潜在特征，这为学习稀疏检索(LSR)提供了自然基础。与现有基于词汇表投影的LSR方法相比，SAE表示有望产生更具语义结构、表达能力更强且语言无关的特征。
